"IBM API Connect could allow a remote attacker to bypass authentication mechanisms and gain unauthorized access to the application," the tech giant said  in a bulletin. <input type='checkbox' name='article_1399' value='https://thehackernews.com/2025/12/researchers-spot-modified-shai-hulud.html' /> 1399 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=thehackernews.com+Researchers+Spot+Modified+Shai-Hulud+Worm+Testing+Payload+on+npm+Registry%EE%A0%82Dec+31%2C+2026Cybersecurity+%2F+MalwareCybersecurity+researchers+have+disclosed+details+of+what+appears+to+be+a+new+strain+of+Shai+Hulud+on+the+npm+registry+with+slight+modifications+from+the+previous+wave+observed+last+month.++The+npm+package+that+embeds+the+novel+Shai+Hulud+strain+is+%22+%40vietmoney%2Freact-big-calendar+%2C%22+which+was+uploaded+to+npm+back+in+March+2021+by+a+user+named+%22hoquocdat.%22+It+was+updated+for+the+first+time+on+December+28%2C+2025%2C+to+version+0.26.2.+The+package+has+been+downloaded+698+times++since+its+initial+publication.+The+latest+version+has+been+downloaded+197+times.++Aikido%2C+which+spotted+the+package%2C+said+it+has+not+spotted+any+major+spread+or+infections+following+the+release+of+the+package.++%22This+suggests+we+may+have+caught+the+attackers+testing+their+payload%2C%22+security+researcher+Charlie+Eriksen+said+.+%22The+differences+in+the+code+suggests+that+this+was+obfuscated+again+from+the+original+source%2C+not+modified+in+place.+This+makes+it+highly+unlikely+to+be+a+copy-ca...' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://thehackernews.com/2025/12/researchers-spot-modified-shai-hulud.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Researchers Spot Modified Shai-Hulud Worm Testing Payload on npm RegistryDec 31, 2026Cybersecurity / MalwareCybersecurity researchers have disclosed details of what appears to be a new strain of Shai Hulud on the npm registry with slight modifications from the previous wave observed last month.  The npm package that embeds the novel Shai Hulud strain is " @vietmoney/react-big-calendar ," which was uploaded to npm back in March 2021 by a user named "hoquocdat." It was updated for the first time on December 28, 2025, to version 0.26.2. The package has been downloaded 698 times  since its initial publication. The latest version has been downloaded 197 times.
